I spent money in a way that really isn’t like me. I bought these expensive light fixtures, some design software add-ons, and even a last-minute flight for a trip I couldn’t really afford. At the time it felt fine, like I’d figure it out, but afterward I realized it had really hit my savings.\n\nDoctor: How long did these periods of feeling almost electric and really upbeat usually last?\n\nPatient: It’s been going on since I moved into this apartment, so… about a month now. The really intense ups and downs have kind of been part of that whole period.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any thoughts or heard any voices that seemed real but weren't based in reality?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I haven’t heard voices or seen things that weren’t there. I’ve had some intense thoughts, but they still felt like my own thoughts, not like something unreal.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts were not your own or that someone or something was controlling your mind?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t felt like someone else was controlling my mind. But then it would flip, and I’d snap at people over really small stuff—like a contractor being delayed, or my sister not responding fast enough. It felt way more intense than my usual mood.\n\nDoctor: Did you have any periods where you didn't need much sleep, yet you still had a lot of energy?\n\nPatient: Yes. For several nights, I only slept maybe three or four hours, but I’d wake up feeling like I was completely fine. I spent money in a way that really isn’t like me. I bought these expensive light fixtures, some design software add-ons, and even a last-minute flight for a trip I couldn’t really afford. At the time it felt fine, like I’d figure it out, but afterward I realized it had really hit my savings.\n\nDoctor: How long did these periods of feeling almost electric and really upbeat usually last?\n\nPatient: It’s been going on since I moved into this apartment, so… about a month now. The really intense ups and downs have kind of been part of that whole period.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any thoughts or heard any voices that seemed real but weren't based in reality?\n\nPatient: No, I don’t think so. I’ve worried what people might think of me, but I haven’t felt like they could actually hear my thoughts.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like your thoughts were being controlled by an outside force?\n\nPatient: No, I haven’t felt like that.
